Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the metals space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 174 and the convergence rate is O(651/n^3).

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to metals theory, we decompose the problem into 174 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 651. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
